Report: Number of Overseas Students Returning to China for Job Hunting in 2022 Hits New High - China News Society
Top 10 Countries of Origin for 2022 Graduates Seeking Jobs Upon Returning Home: Source: "2022 China Overseas Students Employment Survey Report"
China Daily, Beijing, Feb 11 (Meng Rui) — The "2022 China Overseas Returnees Employment Survey Report" (hereinafter referred to as the "Report") released recently shows that the number of graduating overseas students in 2022 increased by 8.6% year-on-year. The number of overseas students seeking jobs upon returning home has set a new record high. At the same time, Chinese enterprises' demand for overseas talents is also on the rise.
The report points out that since 2020, the number of overseas returnees seeking jobs domestically has increased significantly, and the number of recent graduates seeking jobs upon returning to China has also risen.
The number of graduating students studying abroad in 2022 increased by 8.6% compared to 2021, and the number of overseas students returning home to seek jobs has reached a new high.
From the distribution of countries where returning students are seeking jobs abroad, the UK accounts for the highest proportion at 41.4%, followed by Australia (20.3%) and the United States (9%). The ranking is the same as in 2021.
From the perspective of year-on-year growth rates, the number of recent graduates studying abroad from Malaysia, Japan, and Singapore saw significant increases in 2022, with growth rates of 66.1%, 50.9%, and 37.7%, respectively.
The number of recent graduates from Canada, Germany, the United States, and Australia who are applying for jobs has decreased compared to last year.
Chart of the demand and supply trends of overseas returnees in the first week after the Spring Festival in 2023. Source: "Survey Report on the Employment of Chinese Overseas Returnees in 2022"
Surveys show that among overseas students who have decided to return home, 58% believe that "life is more convenient domestically," 40% decided to return because of "greater identification with the domestic cultural environment," 32% think that "there are more job opportunities domestically," and 22% believe that the domestic economy will recover faster, providing more job opportunities.
Data shows that the demand for overseas talents by domestic companies is rapidly increasing, and the demand for returning overseas talents is on the rise.
In the first week after the Spring Festival in 2023, the number of job positions prioritizing overseas returnees and the number of overseas returnees seeking jobs domestically both showed an upward trend. The number of job positions prioritizing overseas returnees on February 3rd increased by 11% compared to January 28th, and the number of overseas returnees seeking jobs domestically increased by 27%.
From an industry perspective, in 2022, the number of positions prioritizing overseas returnees was highest in the education/training industry, accounting for 27.8%, ranking first. The professional services/advisory and internet/e-commerce industries followed with 14.3% and 11.1%, respectively, ranking second and third.
Changes in the number of overseas returnees seeking jobs domestically in recent years. Source: "2022 China Overseas Returnees Employment Survey Report"
From the perspective of compensation, the average recruitment salary for priority positions for overseas returnees has continued to rise for three consecutive years. In 2022, it reached 14,680 RMB per month (the following figures are in RMB), an increase of 961 RMB from 13,719 RMB in 2021, representing a 7% growth.
When looking at regions, in 2022, the willingness of overseas returnees to develop in new first-tier cities, second-tier cities, and third-tier and lower cities all increased. The proportion of job applications in 2022 were 31%, 15.7%, and 14.3%, respectively, which were all higher than those in 2021.
Regarding the benefits of studying abroad, 52.7% of overseas returnees said the experience basically met their expectations, an increase of 2.8 percentage points compared to the previous year (49.9%).
28.2% of overseas returnees said they were higher or much higher than expected, a proportion that is also higher than the 25.4% in 2021.
(End)
Can the cost be reduced by half or even more?
Henan Province standardizes and integrates the pricing for dental implant services - China News Society
CCTV.com News: Dentures are an ideal choice for many patients with missing teeth, but the price of tens of thousands of yuan per tooth has always been a barrier that makes many patients hesitate to take the step.
This year, with the accelerated implementation of centralized procurement for dental implants, various regions have also begun to prioritize price reductions for dental implants.
Starting from February 10th, the comprehensive price control target for the full process of routine dental implant services in Henan Province is 4300 yuan. The total cost of the full process for a single routine dental implant shall not exceed 97% of the control target.
Dental implantation refers to a tooth replacement method where the upper dental prosthesis is supported and retained by a lower structure implanted in the bone tissue for missing teeth.
The staff from Zhengzhou University First Affiliated Hospital said that for a typical single-tooth implant, the price from implantation to final restoration is about 16,000 to 17,000 yuan.
Patient He Ying: After the new policy, the doctor told me that for my condition, the cost is about 8,000 yuan to less than 10,000 yuan, which is basically half the original cost.
On the basis of the current project, Henan Province has also integrated and determined 15 oral implantation service price projects, such as "implantation fee for a single tooth implant." It has uniformly formulated the prices and medical insurance payment categories for public hospitals under provincial management. It has canceled or adjusted the pricing of 20 medical service projects, such as dental implantation surgery.
Li Yunjie, Director of the Department of Medical Price and Bidding Procurement of the Zhengzhou Medical Security Bureau: The main beneficiaries are the four parties involved. For the medical insurance system, the expenditure has decreased. Hospitals have less capital tied up, and patients may see an increase in the number of patients. For suppliers, they have gained a market. For the patients themselves, they are the ones who benefit the most. The cost of planting a single tooth may be reduced by half or even more.
Shenzhen: Building the First in the Country and World-Leading Ultra-Fast Pioneer City - China News Society
China News Agency, Feb 11 (XIN) -- According to a message from the "Shenzhen Industry and Information Technology" WeChat official account on the 10th, the Shenzhen Bureau of Industry and Information Technology has recently issued the "Shenzhen Plan for the Construction of the Fast-Forward Pioneer City."
It proposed that by the end of 2023, a super broadband network with high speed, large capacity, and low latency will be built, achieving the construction goals of "Dual Gigabit, Full Optical Network, 1 millisecond latency, and universal connectivity," and creating a city that is the first in the country and leading globally as a pioneer in ultra-fast connectivity.
The plan proposes to accelerate the upgrading of gigabit optical networks.
By the end of 2023, the proportion of 10G PON ports should reach 100%, and the number of fully optical industrial parks (industrial parks) should exceed 100.
The Plan requires a comprehensive improvement in the energy efficiency of 5G networks.
Research and formulate the service quality standards for Shenzhen's 5G network, conduct 5G network testing and evaluation, and improve the service quality of the 5G network.
Support basic telecommunications enterprises to increase their efforts in optimizing 5G networks in key areas and locations. Aim to add 10,000 new 5G base stations throughout the year. Achieve an average download speed of 500Mbps and an upload speed of 100Mbps across the city. In key areas, achieve a download speed of 1000Mbps and an upload speed of 200Mbps. The network speed and user experience will significantly improve.
Build a high-quality wireless local area network at the city level.
Formulate service standards for wireless local area networks (WLAN) in public places, promote the upgrade of WLAN in areas with dense populations and concentrated information consumption demand across all districts of the city, and provide gigabit access capabilities.
Based on the "Deepi Network" construction, a unified authentication and supervision platform for the city's public wireless local area network is built, realizing "single authentication, seamless roaming, and intelligent switching." It supports operators to include socially built APs (wireless access points) with public service capabilities into the authentication and supervision platform.
By the end of 2023, the total number of APs connected and upgraded should not be less than 20,000.
Moreover, the plan also proposes building a high-quality all-optical transport network, constructing a multi-level computing power supply system, accelerating the construction of key communication network infrastructure, and enhancing the service capabilities of internet applications.
The Plan clearly outlines the coordinated construction of an IoT perception system.
Explore the construction of a city perception terminal management model based on the "OpenHarmony" operating system, reduce the cost of integration and connection, accelerate the construction of a city-level IoT perception platform, and promote the network and data interconnectivity between the city-level IoT platform and various professional IoT platforms in different fields.
Deepen the application of IoT perception in fields such as urban management, people's livelihood services, public safety, and traffic travel. Expand the collection channels of fixed perception such as urban roads, buildings, and public facilities and mobile perception such as intelligent connected vehicles.
By the end of 2023, the number of water, electricity, and gas IoT sensing terminals will exceed 90 million, and more than 20 multi-functional smart pole application demonstration projects will be built.
The plan also mentions strengthening the integration of satellite and ground communication applications, laying out the construction of low-altitude network infrastructure, and promoting deep coverage of 5G networks at sea.
At the same time, promote deep coverage of 5G networks at sea, build an innovative development ecosystem for the industry, and support the hosting of top-tier events and activities.
(China News Agency Economic News)
China's e-commerce industry has seen the emergence of "new variables." How can live-streaming sales achieve "content is king"?
- Xinzhong News Agency
Xinhua News, Feb 11 (Xinhua News Agency) - Title: New Variables Emerging in China's E-commerce Industry: How to Achieve "Content is King" in Live Streaming Sales?
Xia Bin, journalist of Xinzhong Finance
"We entered the e-commerce field relatively late, but from the very beginning, we realized that content is very important if we want to stand out among the countless stores."
"Fangfang, a '90s girl from Guangdong, is now operating a complement and health food store on Douyin E-commerce. She has helped a traditional offline store with a 30-year history 'bloom' online."
Imitating the style of old Hong Kong films and pairing it with classic Cantonese songs, opening @Fangfang's solo dining WeChat account, the exquisite videos are eye-catching and refreshing.
At the "Find Like-Minded People · High-Quality Content Sharing Special Session" of Douyin's E-commerce held recently, Fangfang, as a representative of high-quality creators, attended the event and gave a speech introducing her experiences and insights in daily content creation.
She said on the spot: "The carefully crafted video has attracted a lot of attention from netizens. Many people are interested in the ingredients from my home and want to try making the food together. This has immediately opened up a wide market."
This project requires additional funding, so we need to apply for more funds from the government.
Fangfang's reliance on the soup-making video has made more people love "the taste of old Guangdong." A single live stream can sell up to 300,000 pieces of nourishing ingredients, saving a family's nearly bankrupt old shop.
"No content, no e-commerce." With the continuous development of short video and live streaming e-commerce, the requirements and standards for e-commerce content are entering a new stage.
In the early stages of live streaming e-commerce, live rooms often used low prices and discounts to attract users, and price advantages seemed to be the only selling point at that time.
In recent years, there have been both phenomenon-level influencers such as Liu Fenhong and Dongfang Zhenxuan, as well as many influencers and merchants who have emerged in specialized fields and are not widely known to the general public. They enhance their long-term operating benefits by consistently creating high-quality content that aligns with their brand personality.
High Yaxuan, Director of the Star Authors Operations Department at Douyin E-commerce, introduced that in the past year, 6.27 million creators have turned their content into revenue through Douyin E-commerce. Their content creation volume has increased by more than 252%, and the total transaction amount has also doubled.
"Behind these numbers, we can feel the vibrant and vigorous author ecosystem growing rapidly."
"Gao Yaxuan said."
Previously, the Communication Innovation and Future Media Experimental Platform of Beijing Normal University released the "E-Commerce Industry Content Ecosystem Health Index (ECHI) Report." The report analyzed the importance of "new variables" in the current e-commerce industry—high-quality content—from a data perspective. It used the index to evaluate the content ecosystem situations of 27 major domestic e-commerce platforms.
According to the e-commerce industry content ecosystem health index ranking in the above report, Douyin E-commerce, Kuaishou, Taobao, Pinduoduo, and JD.com are ranked in the top five.
The report believes that the e-commerce industry's content ecosystem is a comprehensive concept, which involves integrating e-commerce with content. However, not all content can be integrated with e-commerce. High-quality content carriers for integration with e-commerce are professional-produced content (PGC) and user-generated content (UGC). The higher the value of the content platform, the more diverse the scenarios for consumption and promotion, and the higher the possibility of e-commerce integration and commercial value. The proportion of e-commerce content production and consumption in the public and private domains will also gradually increase.
On the scene, Professor Yu Guoming, professor of the School of Journalism and Communication at Beijing Normal University and chairman of the China Media Economics and Management Professional Committee, analyzed and interpreted the report.
He said that, in terms of content value, the e-commerce industry has already transitioned from the "accessibility of consumption" stage to the "quality-oriented consumption" stage. High-quality, professional, and differentiated content has become the "hard currency" of the e-commerce industry.
At the same time, content has become the fundamental and key driver of the next stage of e-commerce development. PGC content such as knowledge payment, professional knowledge, and KOLs (key opinion leaders) has begun to become a standard feature in live streaming e-commerce.
Looking ahead to the future development of the e-commerce industry, Yu Guoming put forward two suggestions.
From the perspective of improving the structure of content, it aims to promote the overall improvement of the health level and quality of the e-commerce content ecosystem, build positive content creation and management standards, and achieve a healthy and balanced system index and dynamic equilibrium for the e-commerce industry's content ecosystem.
Another aspect is to look at the external ecosystem. To achieve the healthy development of the content e-commerce industry and realize the value synergy and co-creation with the external environment.
"The e-commerce industry in the future still needs more support in terms of data and artificial intelligence to make predictions for the stability of future development. In addition, it is necessary to provide a more standardized and healthy growth environment for merchants, achieving value synergy among enterprises, users, products, and services within the e-commerce industry, and jointly creating a healthy content ecosystem."
"End"
North China Securities Exchange's Margin Trading and Short Selling Business Will Start Next Week - China News Society
XINHUA REPORT (Reporter Sun Jie) Last night, the Beijing Stock Exchange issued a statement saying that, with the approval of the China Securities Regulatory Commission, the financing and securities lending business of the Beijing Stock Exchange will officially start on February 13. At that time, securities companies that have obtained the financing and securities lending transaction qualifications of the Beijing Stock Exchange can accept investors' entrustments to submit financing and securities lending transaction applications to the Beijing Stock Exchange.
The list of the first 56 securities eligible for margin buying and short selling is determined with January 31, 2023, as the calculation reference date.
The Beijing Stock Exchange stated that the list of securities eligible for margin trading and short selling, as well as the list of securities that can be used as collateral, will be announced through the "Disclosure of Information - Margin Trading and Short Selling Information" column on the Beijing Stock Exchange's official website, making it convenient for investors to query.
Fund lending and securities lending are important foundational systems in the securities market and are relatively mature trading methods in international securities markets.
On September 2 last year, the Beijing Stock Exchange opened for public comments on its margin lending and short selling business as it marked its first anniversary of establishment.
The launch of financing and securities lending transactions at the Beijing Stock Exchange is an important measure to continuously advance the construction of the market system, improve the trading mechanisms, and enhance the pricing functions of the secondary market.
The margin lending and short selling system is conducive to enriching investors' trading strategies, meeting their diverse trading needs, promoting price discovery in the market, and further enhancing market liquidity.
Next, the Beijing Stock Exchange, under the leadership of the China Securities Regulatory Commission, will continue to do a good job in all kinds of guarantee work for the margin trading business. It will enrich the trading system tools in line with investor needs, improve relevant supporting mechanisms, and strive to build a first-rate exchange that serves innovative small and medium-sized enterprises.

It will accelerate the green transformation of the development model, deeply advance pollution prevention and control, comprehensively strengthen resource conservation, and steadily and cautiously promote carbon peaking and carbon neutrality. It will coordinate the promotion of carbon reduction, pollution reduction, green development, and growth, advancing an ecological priority, resource-saving, and green, low-carbon development model.
China is willing to work with Japan to continue deepening cooperation in green and low-carbon industries, technological cooperation, policy dialogue, and people-to-people exchanges, promoting the continuous advancement of green development cooperation between the two countries to new heights.
Japanese Economy and Trade Minister Kazuhiro Saito attended the main forum and delivered a keynote speech.
The forum is co-hosted by the National Development and Reform Commission, Ministry of Commerce of China, the Ministry of Economy and Trade of Japan, and the Japan-China Economic Association. It includes a main forum and four sub-forums on energy efficiency improvement, electric and intelligent automotive technology, hydrogen energy, and long-term Sino-Japanese trade.
Representatives from the governments, businesses, and institutions of China and Japan engaged in exchanges.
Chinese rescue teams are continuing their rescue operations in Turkey, having rescued 4 trapped individuals - China News
China News Agency, February 11 (Xinhua) — According to a message released by the Ministry of Emergency Management on the 11th, on February 10th, the Chinese rescue team currently engaged in search and rescue operations in Turkey sent three action groups with 45 rescuers to rescue one trapped person.
At the same time, two search and evaluation teams were dispatched to conduct an assessment and inspection of the 15 buildings on Woda Bash Street.
As of now, 4 trapped people have been rescued, 5 victims have been found, and life signs and risk assessments have been conducted on 25 severely collapsed buildings.
The rescue team will scientifically adjust the rescue forces based on the actual situation of the disaster area and the on-site conditions, and continue to carry out in-depth rescue operations.
The rescue team obtained rescue request information through various channels such as the embassy, the local government, and UN rescue organizations, and verified each piece of information through methods such as online reconnaissance and on-site search.
Introduce the current rescue environment and the situation of building collapses to the Hong Kong rescue team, and jointly carry out search operations.
Currently responsible for regional coordination (SSC5) in conjunction with the Italian rescue team, and sending 3 members to participate in the work of the 5th regional rescue coordination center. Communications have been carried out with multiple teams including Italy, the UK, Serbia, Slovakia, and Oman, understanding the rescue work situation of the United Nations rescue organization and rescue teams from various countries.
The rescue team continues to strengthen safety management and team support. Following the principle of "unified command and safety first," they are enhancing safety control at the rescue site and reinforcing discipline requirements for rescue operations, clearly defining safety precautions.
Continuously implement the system of rotating duty and sentry posts, and strengthen the safety management of the camp area.
The medical staff of the rescue team provide daily physical examinations for the members, strengthen the medical security of key personnel, and currently all members of the rescue team are in good health. They are conducting pest control in the camp area.
After the self-established base was set up, the logistics support personnel collectively erected 26 temporary tents, divided into four categories: command and communication, equipment storage, medical decontamination, living quarters and dog houses. These tents ensured the availability of more than 30 sets of rescue equipment on site.
Xinhua Commentator: Advancing China's Modernization in Its Own Way Is a Systemic Project — Fourth Perspective: Deeply Grasp Comrade Xi Jinping's Important Speech at the Opening Ceremony of the Study Seminar on Studying and Implementing the Spirit of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China — China News Service
Xinhua News Agency, Beijing, February 11. (Title: Advancing the Modernization of China in a Chinese Way is a Systemic Project —— The Fourth Perspective on Deeply Understanding President Xi Jinping's Important Speech at the Opening Ceremony of the Study Session on Carrying Out the Study of the Spirit of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China)
Xinhua Commentator
"Promoting the modernization of China in its own way is a systematic project that requires comprehensive coordination, overall planning, and coordinated advancement. It is necessary to correctly handle a series of major relationships, such as the relationship between top-level design and practical exploration, strategy and tactics, upholding the correct path and innovation, efficiency and fairness, vitality and order, as well as self-reliance and strength with openness to the outside world."
"At the opening ceremony of the study session for new members and alternate members of the Central Committee and provincial and ministerial-level leading cadres to learn and implement the thought of Xi Jinping in the new era of socialism with Chinese characteristics and the spirit of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China, President Xi Jinping scientifically grasps the laws of modernization construction, thoroughly and systematically elaborates a series of major relationships that need to be handled in practice, providing a scientific methodology for vigorously promoting the modernization of China with Chinese characteristics."
Advancing the modernization of China in its own way involves various fields such as economy, politics, culture, society, and ecological civilization. It relates to all aspects of governing the Party, the country, and the military, as well as domestic, foreign, and defense affairs. We need to make systematic plans and be brave in reform and innovation, continuously achieving overall progress.
Carrying out top-level design requires a deep understanding of the grand trends of world development, accurate grasp of the common aspirations of the people, and in-depth exploration of the laws of economic and social development. The formulated plans and policy systems should reflect the era, grasp the laws, and be creative, achieving a combination of near and far-term considerations, vertical and horizontal coordination, and coherent content.
Advancing the modernization with Chinese characteristics is an exploratory endeavor. There are still many unknown areas, and we need to boldly explore them in practice. We should promote the development of the cause through reform and innovation. We must not stick to old methods, nor wait idly for a rabbit to fall into the trap.
Strategic issues are fundamental problems for a party and a country.
Advancing the modernization of China with Chinese characteristics requires enhancing the strategic foresight to use scientific strategies to foresee and lead the future; it requires enhancing the strategic comprehensiveness, focusing on solving major issues that are critical to the success or failure of the Party and state's cause, where one link affects the whole; and it requires enhancing the strategic stability. Once a strategy is formulated, it must be consistently upheld, resolutely implemented, and ultimately achieved successfully.
It is necessary to scientifically grasp the dialectical unity of strategy and tactics, combine the principled nature of strategy with the flexibility of tactics, be flexible and adaptable, make decisions on the spot, and respond promptly. By adapting to local conditions, responding to changes, and acting with the trend, we can grasp strategic initiative.
The Chinese nation is a nation that upholds the correct path and promotes innovation.
思考一下，用户需要将这段中文翻译成英文。首先，我需要通读原文，理解每个部分的意思。 第一句：“处理好守正与创新的关系”可以翻译为“Handle the relationship between upholding the correct path and innovation.”或者“Address the balance between preserving the essence and promoting innovation.” 接下来，“既要守好中国式现代化的本和源、根和魂”这部分可能需要更准确的表达，比如“we must firmly uphold the fundamental nature and essential characteristics of China's modernization.”或者“we need to safeguard the root and essence of China's modernization.” “毫不动摇坚持中国式现代化的中国特色、本质要求、重大原则”这部分可能需要拆分成几个短语，比如“unwaveringly adhere to the distinctive features, essential requirements, and major principles of China's modernization.” “确保中国式现代化的正确方向”可以译为“ensure the correct direction of China's modernization.” 然后，“又要把创新摆在国家发展全局的突出位置”可能翻译为“we must place innovation at the core of the country's overall development strategy.” “顺应时代发展要求，着眼于解决重大理论和实践问题”这部分可能需要处理为“align with the demands of the times and focus on addressing major theoretical and practical issues.” “积极识变应变求变，大力推进改革创新”可以译为“actively adapt to changes, respond to them, and seek new ways forward, vigorously promoting reform and innovation.” “不断塑造发展新动能新优势”可能需要表达为“continuously foster new sources of development momentum and new advantages.” “充分激发全社会创造活力”可以译为“fully unleash the creative vitality of society as a whole.” 现在需要把这些部分组合成一个连贯的英文段落，确保准确传达原意，同时保持语言流畅。可能需要调整句子结构，使整体更自然。 比如： "Handle the relationship between upholding the essential principles and promoting innovation. We should actively adapt to changes, respond to them, and seek new ways forward, vigorously promoting reform and innovation. We must continuously foster new sources of development momentum and new advantages, fully unleashing the creative vitality of society as a whole." 或者更简洁一些： "Balancing the preservation of the essence with innovation, we must firmly uphold the fundamental nature and core essence of China's modernization, unwaveringly adhere to its distinctive features, essential requirements, and major principles to ensure the correct direction of China's modernization. (Germany's Radio Germany Chinese website) The Biden administration added six Chinese entities suspected of participating in the spy balloon plan to the blacklist this Friday (February 10).
The U.S. Department of Commerce said that five Chinese companies and one research institution have helped modernize the Chinese military, particularly in aerospace projects, including airships and aircraft.
Companies listed on the blacklist find it difficult to obtain U.S. technology exports.
The list is known as the "entity list." Companies listed on the list will be unable to purchase technology or goods from U.S. suppliers unless they obtain a special export license from the U.S. Department of Commerce.
Companies listed on the blacklist are: Beijing Nanjiang Aerospace Science and Technology Co., Ltd., No. 48 Research Institute of China Electronics Technology Group, Dongguan Lingkong Remote Sensing Technology Co., Ltd., Yingmen Aerospace Technology Group Co., Ltd., Guangzhou Tianhai Xiang Aerospace Technology Co., Ltd., and Shandong Yingmen Aerospace Technology Group Co., Ltd.
Previously, the White House said it was considering taking broader measures to "expose and address" China's larger-scale surveillance activities that pose a threat to U.S. national security.
The balloon incident has further heightened tensions between the United States and China, leading to the cancellation of Secretary of State Blinken's visit to China.
The United States shot down a balloon on February 4. The Chinese government seriously lodged an objection with the U.S. embassy in China, criticizing the United States for seriously undermining and damaging the efforts and processes of both sides to stabilize and advance Sino-U.S. relations.
From the existing wreckage evidence and information on the Chinese military's procurement platform, the U.S. government has drawn the conclusion that the manufacturer of the balloon has a direct connection with the Chinese People's Liberation Army, and that it is a supplier approved by the Chinese military.
China's Foreign Ministry Spokesperson Mao Ning said at a Friday press conference that China has already explained the situation and stated its position on the incident where a civilian balloon from China accidentally entered U.S. airspace due to unavoidable circumstances.
According to the Foreign Ministry website, she added that the relevant resolutions of the U.S. Congress are purely political maneuvering and hype.
The Chinese side is strongly dissatisfied and firmly opposes this.
Assistant: Assistant: U.S. Commerce Department Deputy Undersecretary for Export Enforcement Matthew Axelrod said, "Today's actions demonstrate the collective effort we have made to identify and prevent China from using spy balloons that have invaded the airspace of the United States and 40 other countries."
This project requires more financial support, so we need to apply for additional funds from the government.
Reuters reported that an employee of Guangzhou Tianhaihang Aviation Technology Co., Ltd. said he was unaware of the sanctions and refused to comment further.
The Chinese embassy in the United States has not yet commented on this information.

(Germany's Radio Deutschland Chinese Website) Several days have passed since the earthquake struck parts of Turkey and Syria, and the hope of finding survivors has become increasingly slim. However, a family of five who were rescued on Saturday brought immense comfort to the search and rescue personnel.
According to a report by AP, a five-member family trapped under the ruins in the heavily affected Nurdagi town in Gaziantep province was successfully rescued more than five days after being trapped.
First, the search and rescue personnel pulled out the mother and daughter from the pile of ruins. Then they found the father, but he insisted on saving his son and the other daughter first.
After everyone was rescued, cheers erupted among the crowd.
In southern Turkey's city of Kahramanmaraş, a 16-year-old boy and a 70-year-old elderly woman were also successfully rescued under harsh cold weather conditions.
"What day is today?"
—"This 12-year-old boy, who had been trapped beneath the ruins for 129 hours, asked the search and rescue personnel who had saved him when he was brought back to the surface."
A man from Kahramanmaraş is holding the cat he just rescued.
A miraculous rescue case brought a glimmer of hope to the already desperate search and rescue efforts, but at the same time, the number of victims was continuously rising.
According to official statistics, the earthquake on Monday has already caused more than 20,000 deaths in Turkey, with the death toll in neighboring Syria estimated to exceed 3,500.
Although experts say that people can survive for more than a week after being trapped under the ruins, the hope of finding more survivors has become increasingly slim.
Rescue personnel are still using thermal imaging cameras to search for signs of life beneath the ruins.
At the same time, the focus of the rescue efforts began to shift towards helping the survivors who needed immediate assistance.
UN officials: "A century-unprecedented" disaster in the region
Undersecretary-General for Humanitarian Affairs and Emergency Relief Coordinator Martin Griffiths said at a press conference in Turkey that this earthquake is "the worst disaster in the region in a century."
He also praised Turkey's response to the disaster as "unusual."
He also told Reuters that he hopes to provide aid to areas controlled by the Syrian government and opposition groups, but also noted that the matter "is still unclear."
The United Nations has pledged to provide an additional $25 million in aid donations to the people affected by the earthquake in Syria.
This week earlier, the United Nations has already announced a donation of 25 million dollars for disaster relief in Turkey and Syria.
The most destructive earthquake in the world in the past 60 years
1960 Chilean Earthquake
At 3:11 p.m. local time on May 22, 1960, a earthquake with a magnitude of 9.5 on the moment magnitude scale was recorded in Valdivia, Chile, making it the largest earthquake ever observed in human history.
The entire city of Valdivia turned into ruins within just ten minutes.
The massive tsunami triggered by the earthquake hit the Chilean coastline, with waves reaching a height of 25 meters.
The tsunami affected distant Japan and the Philippines.
The exact number of casualties and economic losses caused by such a widespread disaster cannot be precisely determined.
The most destructive earthquake in the world in the past 60 years
Alaska Earthquake (1964)
March 27, 1964 was a Friday, which was also Good Friday.
On this day, a earthquake of magnitude 9.2 occurred in Alaska, USA, making it the largest earthquake in North American history.
Until 2006, the Alaskan earthquake was still the third largest earthquake ever recorded in the world.
The fault between the Pacific Plate and the North American Plate broke near College Inlet in Prince William Sound during the earthquake.
In most areas, the earthquake lasted between 3 and 5 minutes.
The tsunami caused by the sea bed displacement reached a height of 67 meters.
Since it was a holiday, the school and many companies were closed.
This earthquake ultimately resulted in 139 deaths.
Nine people died directly from the earthquake, and another 130 died from the tsunami caused by the earthquake.
The most destructive earthquake in the world in the past 60 years
1976 Tangshan Earthquake
On July 28, 1976, two strong earthquakes of magnitude 7 or higher struck the Tangshan City, Fengnan County, Hebei Province, China within 16 hours.
The hypocenter is 23 kilometers below the surface.
The entire city of Tangshan was suddenly reduced to ruins, with all traffic, communication, water supply, and power supply systems cut off.
The capital Beijing, 140 kilometers away, also felt strong tremors.
According to official statistics, including Beijing and Tianjin, all the disaster-stricken areas suffered 242,769 deaths and 164,851 seriously injured people due to the earthquake.
Taiyuan Earthquake is the second deadliest earthquake in China with recorded history, ranking behind the 1556 Jiangli Earthquake.
However, overseas scientists estimate that this earthquake may cause the death of at least 650,000 people.
The most destructive earthquake in the world in the past 60 years
2004 Indian Ocean Earthquake
On December 26, 2004, an earthquake occurred 160 kilometers off the western coast of Aceh Province, Sumatra Island, Indonesia. The earthquake had a depth of 30 kilometers and a moment magnitude of 9.1 to 9.3.
The earthquake triggered a massive tsunami with wave heights reaching 15 to 30 meters.
A total of 14 countries along the Pacific coast were affected, with the most severely affected areas including Aceh Province in Indonesia, the Phuket and Krabi tourist areas in Thailand, Sri Lanka, and the Tamil Nadu region in India.
At that time, it was the peak season for Christmas tourism, and a large number of tourists and local residents had gathered in the disaster area. This led to many tourists enjoying their holidays on the beach and local workers working by the sea being swept under the sea by the tsunami, making many people disappear as a result of the earthquake.
It is estimated that the number of victims and missing persons is at least 300,000.
The most destructive earthquake in the world in the past 60 years
Wenchuan Earthquake (2008)
On May 12, 2008, a major earthquake struck Wenchuan, Sichuan Province, China.
According to data from the China Earthquake Administration, the surface wave magnitude of this earthquake reached 8.2, and the moment magnitude reached 8.3. The area affected by the disaster exceeded 100,000 square kilometers.
As of September 25, 2008, the earthquake caused a total of 69,227 deaths, 17,923 missing persons, 374,643 injured people, and 19.93 million people to lose their homes. The total number of people affected by the disaster reached 46.256 million.
This is the most destructive earthquake since the founding of the People's Republic of China, and also the most deadly one after the Tangshan Earthquake.
In March 2009, the Chinese State Council announced that May 12th each year would be designated as Disaster Prevention and Mitigation Day, to strengthen public education and attention on disaster prevention and mitigation work.
After that, governments in many places in China and primary and secondary schools hold earthquake drills on this day every year.
Primary and secondary schools have also begun to pay attention to popularizing earthquake prevention and disaster reduction education.
The most destructive earthquake in the world in the past 60 years
2010 Haitian earthquake
On January 12, 2010, a 7.0 magnitude earthquake struck Haiti, a Caribbean island nation.
The epicenter was located about 25 kilometers southwest of Port-au-Prince, the capital of Haiti, and the hypocenter was approximately 13 kilometers below the surface.
The International Red Cross estimates that the population affected by the earthquake is approximately 3 million.
The Haitian government has announced that the number of confirmed deaths exceeds 200,000.
In some areas within Haiti, as many as 90% of the houses were destroyed in the earthquake.
Although the international community has provided a large amount of aid and support, this poor country, which is plagued by overpopulation and corruption, has never recovered from this disaster.
The most destructive earthquake in the world in the past 60 years
2011 Tohoku Earthquake
The March 11, 2011 earthquake off the northeastern coast of Japan was a large reverse fault earthquake with a magnitude of 9.0.
The epicenter was located in the Pacific Ocean east of the capital of Miyagi Prefecture, Sendai City, with a hypocenter depth of 24 kilometers, and it triggered a massive tsunami with a maximum run-up height of 40.1 meters.
This earthquake was the first one with a magnitude exceeding 9 recorded in Japan's history.
About 22,000 people died in the disaster, and approximately 400,000 buildings were completely destroyed or collapsed.
The Fukushima Daiichi Nuclear Power Plant suffered a leakage accident due to the impact of an earthquake and tsunami.
This incident was classified as the most severe level 7 on the International Nuclear Event Scale (INES).
The Fukushima nuclear accident caused trace amounts of radioactive substances, including iodine-131 and cesium-137 (with a half-life of 30 years), to be measured worldwide.
Therefore, a large amount of radioactive isotopes were released into the Pacific Ocean due to the nuclear accident.
According to UN statistics, at least 870,000 people in the disaster areas of Turkey and Syria currently urgently need food.
As for Syria alone, more than 5.3 million people have been displaced due to the earthquake.
The World Food Programme (WFP) has called for a donation of 77 million dollars to provide food for displaced people in Turkey and Syria affected by the earthquake.
Currently, temperatures in the disaster-stricken area have dropped as low as below zero, and many displaced people have no shelter.
The Turkish government has distributed a large number of hot meals, tents, and blankets, but these supplies still find it difficult to reach many people in urgent need of assistance.
Meanwhile, in Syria, which has been trapped in a civil war for more than 12 years, the earthquake has further exacerbated the suffering of the people.
Within four days of the earthquake, the World Food Programme has provided food aid to 115,000 people in Turkey and Syria.
The World Health Organization (WHO) sent 72 tons of medical supplies for trauma care and emergency surgery.
(AP, Reuters, etc.)

NEWS GOV-MO: Police Notice: The Police Bureau urges citizens to be wary of fraudulent short messages from telecom companies
Recently, the police department received multiple reports of telecom fraud cases. Unscrupulous individuals have impersonated the Macau Telecommunications Company and sent fraudulent text messages to victims, containing links to websites. They lure victims into logging into fake websites to exchange points by claiming that the points are about to expire and that they need to redeem them promptly.
The victim entered the credit card details and verification code as instructed. Soon after, they received a notification from the financial institution about unauthorized consumption records on their credit card, suspecting that the credit card information had been stolen through the aforementioned website.
According to the investigation, it has been confirmed that the relevant telecommunications company did not send the aforementioned short message.
The police bureau urges citizens to remain vigilant if they receive similar text messages on their phones. They should not click on the links in the messages or provide any personal information. If asked to provide credit card details, account transaction passwords, or transaction verification codes, they should be especially cautious to prevent credit card theft.
Fraud Prevention Reminder: Do not easily believe calls, text messages, or online information from strangers. Be alert to fraud.
1. Pay more attention to cybersecurity information and enhance personal prevention awareness;
2. Do not log in to websites you do not know the source of and register your personal and credit card information on them, to prevent your information from being stolen;
3. Be cautious of fake websites and phishing sites, and always check whether the website URL is exactly the same as the official website;
4. Install antivirus software on your phone, mobile device, or computer and update it regularly;
5. If you suspect you have encountered fraud or other criminal activities, contact the police department's anti-fraud inquiry hotline 8800 7777 or the reporting hotline 993 for help immediately.
NEWS GOV-MO: Hong Kong Large Delegation Visits Tourism Attractions in Macau, Attracting Hong Kong Tourists and Expanding the "Joint Travel" Cooperation Between Hong Kong and Macau
The tourism bureau organized a large-scale tour group of more than 100 people, consisting of representatives from the Hong Kong government's tourism department, the tourism industry and the media, to Macau. The two-day itinerary (on the 10th and 11th) included meetings and industry discussions. Hong Kong travel agencies' industry representatives and media members also visited multiple tourist attractions, hotels, and tourism-related facilities in Macau in batches. This aimed to introduce the latest Macau tourism products to the Hong Kong market and promote cultural and tourism cooperation between the two places. The initiative also sought to jointly expand the "linked travel" market between Hong Kong and Macau.
In-person visits to Macau introduce new tourism experiences
This large group of 100 people from Hong Kong visited Macau for exchange and study. The group included leaders and officials from Hong Kong's tourism administrative departments, more than 60 representatives from travel agencies and cross-border transportation operators in Hong Kong and Macau, and about 30 journalists from travel magazines and newspapers, as well as representatives from tourism social media, online media, bloggers, and influencers. They visited Macau to learn about its latest tourism resources. The Hong Kong tourism industry and media will bring Macau's new tourism experiences to Hong Kong residents and visitors to Hong Kong. By leveraging the advantages of Hong Kong's transportation hubs, they will expand the "linked tourism" market between Hong Kong and Macau.

Attracting Hong Kong tourists and expanding the "joint tour" between Hong Kong and Macau

Following last month's invitation of Hong Kong tourism industry leaders to Australia for exchange and inspection, and the experience of Hong Kong netizens in Macau's "Tourism Plus" initiative, this time the organization has again arranged for Hong Kong tourism industry and media representatives to visit Australia for inspection. This move aims to strengthen market promotion efforts and attract Hong Kong tourists to stay and spend in Australia. The two sides have also discussed cooperating to expand the "linked tourism" market between Hong Kong and Macau, jointly exploring markets in mainland China and overseas, and promoting the recovery of the tourism industry in both regions.
Xi Jinping Writes Back to Encourage Chinese Medical Team Members Aiding in Africa to Use Their Compassion and Medical Skills to Benefit the Local People and to Tell the Story of China Through Their Actions - Rolling News - Hunan Daily Network - Huasheng Online
Xi Jinping writes back to encourage Chinese medical team members who are serving in the Democratic Republic of the Congo to use their compassion and medical skills to benefit the local people, and to tell the story of China with their practical actions.
2023-02-11 13:09:10 [Source: Xinhua News Agency] [Editor: Li Xufeng] Font: 【Large Medium Small】
Xinhua News Agency, Beijing, February 10 — President Xi Jinping, General Secretary of the CPC Central Committee, President of the State, and Chairman of the Central Military Commission, wrote a letter to the members of the 19th batch of Chinese medical teams assisting the Central African Republic on February 9. He conveyed his greetings to them and to all the medical workers sent abroad, and expressed his sincere expectations for them.
Xi Jinping wrote in his letter that your efforts to overcome difficulties in work and life and to serve the local people with sincerity are both life-saving and healing "white angels" and friendly messengers who convey goodwill to the people of China and Africa.
This year marks the 60th anniversary of the dispatch of Chinese medical teams for foreign aid. I extend my sincere greetings and congratulations to those who are currently and have previously been engaged in overseas medical missions.
Xi Jinping pointed out that the Chinese people love peace and value life, and overseas medical aid is a vivid example of this.
嗯，用户让我把这段话翻译成英文。首先，我需要仔细阅读原文，理解每个部分的意思。 第一句：“希望你们不忘初心、牢记使命，大力弘扬不畏艰苦、甘于奉献、救死扶伤、大爱无疆的中国医疗队精神。”这里有几个关键点：不忘初心、牢记使命，这是常见的政治用语，可能翻译成“stay true to our original aspiration and remember our mission”或者“never forget our original aspirations and always bear in mind our mission”。“大力弘扬”可以是“greatly promote”或者“ vigorously promote”。“不畏艰苦”是“dare to face hardships”或者“unyielding in the face of difficulties”。“甘于奉献”是“willing to make sacrifices”或者“willing to dedicate oneself”。“救死扶伤”是“save lives and heal the wounded”或者“save lives and treat injuries”。“大爱无疆”可能需要意译，比如“boundless love”或者“selfless love without limits”。 接下来第二句：“以仁心仁术造福当地人民，以实际行动讲好中国故事。”“以仁心仁术”可以是“with the spirit of benevolence and skill”或者“with the virtue of benevolence and the skill of medicine”。“造福当地人民”是“benefit the people of the local area”或者“benefit the local population”。“以实际行动讲好中国故事”可能翻译为“through concrete actions to tell the story of China well”或者“by taking practical actions to tell China's story well”。 第三句：“为推动构建人类卫生健康共同体作出更大贡献。”这里“推动构建”是“promote the construction of”或者“push forward the building of”。“人类卫生健康共同体”可能需要译为“a community with a shared future in human health and well-being”或者“a community of shared health and well-being for humanity”。“作出更大贡献”是“make greater contributions”或者“contribute more significantly”。 现在需要把这些部分组合起来，确保语句通顺，符合英文表达习惯。可能需要调整结构，比如： "Hope you will stay true to your original aspirations and remember your mission, and vigorously promote the spirit of the Chinese medical team characterized by daring to face hardships, willing to make sacrifices, saving lives and healing the wounded, and boundless love. Use your benevolent hearts and skills to benefit the local people, and through concrete actions tell the story of China well. Make greater contributions to promoting the building of a community with a shared future in human health and well-being." 或者更简洁一些： "I hope you will never forget your original aspirations and always bear in mind your mission, and vigorously promote the spirit of the Chinese medical team, which is characterized by facing hardships bravely, being willing to make sacrifices, saving lives and healing the wounded, and having boundless love. Use your benevolent hearts and skills to benefit the local people, and through concrete actions tell the story of China well. Make greater contributions to promoting the building of a community with a shared future in human health and well-being." 可能需要进一步润色，比如“大爱无疆”可能更准确的是“boundless love”或者“selfless love without limits”。“救死扶伤”是“saving lives and healing the wounded”或者“saving lives and treating injuries”。“中国医疗队精神”可以译为“the spirit of the Chinese medical team”或者“the spirit of the Chinese medical contingent”。 综合以上，最终的翻译可能是： "I hope you will never forget your original aspirations and always remember your mission, and vigorously promote the spirit of the Chinese medical team characterized by courage in the face of hardship, willingness to make sacrifices, saving lives and healing the wounded, and boundless love.
