Foreign medical aid is an important part of China's foreign aid work.
In 1963, our country sent its first medical team abroad to Algeria.
Over the past 60 years, more than 30,000 medical workers have been sent to 76 countries and regions in Africa, Asia, the Americas, Europe, and Oceania, treating 290 million patients, and have won widespread praise from the international community.
Currently, overseas medical teams are working at 115 medical posts in 56 countries around the world, with nearly half of them located in remote and harsh areas.
Recently, 11 members of the 19th batch of Chinese medical teams sent to the Central African Republic wrote a letter to President Xi Jinping, reporting on their study and implementation of the spirit of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China, their provision of medical services to local people, and expressing their determination to contribute to the construction of a community with a shared future in human health and well-being.
Graduation of the Study Class for Newly-Elected Members and Alternate Members of the Central Committee and Provincial and Ministerial-Level Principal Officials on Learning and Implementing the Thought of Xi Jinping in the New Era and the Spirit of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China - Rolling News - Hunan Daily Network - Huasheng Online
Graduation of the Study Class for Newly-Elected Members and Alternate Members of the Central Committee and Provincial and Ministerial-Level Principal Officials on Learning and Implementing the Thought of Xi Jinping in the New Era and the Spirit of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China
2023-02-11 13:07:20 [Source: Xinhua News Agency] [Editor: Li Xufeng] Font: 【Large Medium Small】
Xinhua News, Beijing, February 10 — The study session for new members and alternate members of the Central Committee, as well as provincial and ministerial-level leading cadres, to learn and understand the thought of Xi Jinping in the new era and the spirit of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China concluded on the afternoon of February 10 at the Central Party School (National Academy of Governance). At the closing ceremony, Cai Qi, a member of the Standing Committee of the Political Bureau of the CPC Central Committee and deputy secretary of the Secretariat of the CPC Central Committee, attended and delivered a summary speech.
He said that we should thoroughly study and implement the important speech by President Xi Jinping at the opening ceremony of the study group. We should be more firmly committed to upholding the "two establishments" and resolutely achieving the "two safeguards." We should enhance our capabilities, work diligently and practically, and actively promote all the work related to the modernization of China's development. We should create achievements that can stand the test of history, practice, and the people in the process of fully building a modern socialist country and advancing the great rejuvenation of the Chinese nation.
During the training session, all participants took Xi Jinping's Thought on Socialism with Chinese Characteristics for a New Era as their guiding principle, carefully studied the spirit of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China and Xi Jinping's important speech at the opening ceremony of the training session. They deeply discussed and exchanged views in relation to their actual work, further enhancing their understanding of a series of major theoretical and practical issues concerning the modernization of China's style. They also strengthened their confidence and determination to advance the great rejuvenation of the Chinese nation through the modernization of China under the strong leadership of the Central Committee of the CPC with Xi Jinping at its core. They also enhanced their sense of responsibility and mission to perform their duties, do a good job, and ensure a good start for a new journey of the times.
The 10 representatives of the study group delivered speeches at the graduation ceremony, reporting on and exchanging their learning experiences and gains.
Cai Qi said that studying and implementing the thought of Xi Jinping in the new era of socialism with Chinese characteristics and the spirit of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China is not only the primary political task at present and for the next period, but also a long-term strategic task. It is necessary to follow the requirements of "comprehensively studying, comprehensively grasping, and comprehensively implementing" to promote the work of studying and implementing continuously deeper, more practical, and more heartfelt.
It is necessary to have a deeper understanding of the decisive significance of upholding the "two establishments," firmly maintain the core position of General Secretary Xi Jinping, uphold the authority and centralized, unified leadership of the Central Committee of the CPC, continuously improve political judgment, political understanding and political implementation ability, and always maintain high consistency with the Central Committee of the CPC with Comrade Xi Jinping at its core in thought, politics and action.
We must strive to master Marxism as our fundamental ability. We should consciously use the innovative theories of the Party to arm our minds, guide our practices, and promote our work. We should focus on deep study, careful understanding, and determined implementation, truly learning to foster firm beliefs, absolute loyalty, and a sense of mission and responsibility.
We must thoroughly implement the various decisions and deployments of the 20th National Congress of the Communist Party of China with a spirit of striving forward and being proactive. We should vigorously promote the modernization with Chinese characteristics, work hard with both hands, and take on the role of "project managers" to diligently turn the blueprint into reality step by step.
We must always remember that comprehensive and strict governance of the Party is a long-term and arduous task, and that the self-revolution of the Party is also a long-term and arduous task. We should firmly shoulder the political responsibility of comprehensive and strict governance of the Party, and promote the great project of Party building in the new era with the political construction of the Party as the leading thread.
We need to enhance the ability to promote high-quality development, serve the people, and prevent and resolve risks. We should vigorously promote the style of investigation and research, and firmly prevent and overcome formalism and bureaucratism.
Shi Taiping, Li Ganjie, Li Shulei, Chen Wenqing, Liu Jin guo, Wang Xiaohong attended the closing ceremony, and Chen Xi presided over the closing ceremony.
Google AI chatbot "gets questions wrong" parent company's stock value plummets by trillions of dollars
(Xinhua News Agency, Comprehensive 11th News)
Google's new artificial intelligence chatbot, Bard, made a poor debut when it "got questions wrong" in its promotional video, causing the stock price of Alphabet, Google's parent company, to drop as much as 8.9% on Wednesday, breaking below the $100 mark and losing $110 billion in market value (about $476.5 billion). As of Friday's closing price, it was reported at $94.57 per share.
Market worries that Alphabet is falling behind its competitor Microsoft.
According to a report by Reuters, Google had earlier released a promotional video for its AI chatbot Bard on social media. In the video, someone asked Bard: "What new discoveries about the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) can I tell my 9-year-old child?"
"Bard mentioned in its response that the telescope captured the first photo of an exoplanet."
However, according to NASA's data, the first photograph of an exoplanet was taken by the Very Large Telescope at the European Southern Observatory in 2004.
The Reuters article is titled "Alphabet's Stock Plummets After Bard Gives Wrong Answer".
On the other hand, Google pointed out that Bard's response "highlighted the importance of rigorous testing processes." It will combine external feedback with its own internal testing to ensure that Bard's responses "meet high standards based on quality, safety, and factual information."
Singapore's tourism industry is recovering strongly, with inbound tourists increasing 19 times compared to 2021.
(Xinhua, Nov. 11) The Singapore Tourism Board recently announced that it is expected to welcome 12 million to 14 million international visitors to Singapore in 2023, bringing tourism revenue of about S$18 billion to S$21 billion; the number of visitors and tourism revenue are expected to recover to about 67% to 75% of the levels before the pandemic in 2019. There is a high likelihood that the tourism industry will fully recover in 2024.
The Singapore tourism market gradually resumed and recovered in 2022.
The latest statistics released by the Singapore Tourism Board show that the number of inbound tourists in 2022 increased 19 times compared to 2021, rising from 3.06 million to 6.3 million. This exceeded the tourism board's earlier forecast of 4 to 6 million. Tourists have already recovered to 33% of pre-pandemic levels.
From January to September 2022, Singapore's tourism revenue reached 896 million Singapore dollars. It is estimated that the total tourism revenue for the entire year of 2022 will reach 13.8 to 14.3 billion Singapore dollars, a growth of 6 to 7 times compared to the 189 million Singapore dollars in 2021. This has already recovered to about 50% to 52% of the level before the pandemic in 2019.
The Singapore government is taking multiple measures to accelerate the recovery of the tourism sector.
In April 2022, the government announced a nearly 500 million new dollar funding allocation to stimulate the recovery of the tourism industry.
The tourism bureau announced recently that it plans to allocate 110 million new dollars from this budget over the next two years to launch more business and leisure activities to attract tourists to return.
To make healthy living a key attraction of Singapore's tourism, the Singapore Tourism Board launched an intent-seeking campaign on November 23, 2022, planning to develop a health and wellness destination along the southern coast of Singapore.
Wang Lingli, Director of the Singapore Tourist Board's Office for Sports and Wellness Tourism, said that in recent years, an increasing number of tourists have placed their health and well-being at the top of their consideration factors.
Developing a health and wellness attraction in the Marina Bay area, which includes Marina Bay Gardens and Marina Bay Promenade, is expected to contribute to Singapore's tourism and economy by encouraging tourists to spend more time and money in the country.
Chen Jianlong, the Director of Tourism for Singapore, said that health and wellness will become an important component of Singapore's tourism products in the coming years.
The COVID-19 pandemic has raised public awareness and demand for physical and mental health.
For this purpose, the tourism bureau has been promoting health and wellness programs to both local residents and international tourists over the past two years.
The Singapore Tourism Board held the first Healthy Living Festival in June 2022, offering the public over 130 health-related activities and experiences.
Singapore will continue to offer tourists new travel experiences.
The tour bureau will support the development and updates of projects such as the Avatar World in Gardens by the Bay, the Sentosa Central Beach Market, the newly added "Exploring New Frontiers" exhibition at the National Museum of Singapore, and the open-air theater at the Night Safari, as well as the Bird Park at Wild Life Singapore. This will help make Singapore an attractive destination for international travelers.
According to the plan, Singapore will establish industry standards related to the sustainable development of meetings, exhibitions, and incentive tourism within the year 2023. The focus will be on improving waste management and carbon emissions, further optimizing the tourism environment.
The recovery of Singapore's tourism industry has accelerated the recovery of the aviation industry.
The data shows that as of December 18, 2022, the passenger traffic at Changi Airport has recovered strongly. The airport welcomed as many as 150,000 passengers per day, totaling about 1 million per week, which is roughly 75% of the level before the pandemic. The number of aviation industry employees on the job has also recovered to about 90% of the pre-pandemic level.
According to the data, from January to October 2022, Singapore received 23.6 million tourists, equivalent to 42% of the pre-pandemic level, and 1.7 million flights took off and landed, equivalent to 53% of the pre-pandemic level.
The rapid recovery of the tourism industry has also spurred the recovery of sectors such as accommodation, transportation, catering, and shopping.
The Singapore government and tourism industry are highly optimistic about the Chinese market.
Co Suhu, deputy director of the International Department of the Singapore Tourist Board, said that a survey by a Chinese travel platform showed that Singapore's safety index ranks among the top three globally, which is also one of the reasons why Singapore is popular among Chinese tourists.
Before the outbreak of the pandemic, about 3.6 million Chinese people traveled to Singapore for tourism and leisure each year.
With China easing its border policies, it is expected that the number of Chinese tourists this year will recover to 30% to 60% of that in 2019.
Ko Shudan pointed out that next, the tourism bureau will closely monitor the further signals of China's policies on outbound travel, to respond to changes in tourist demand.
The Singapore Tourism Board will not only strengthen partnerships with online travel platforms and travel agencies such as Ctrip in China, but also actively prepare to welcome Chinese tourists, taking into account the new trends in outbound tourism among Chinese travelers outside of China.
Looking ahead to the 2023 tourism market, the Singapore Tourism Board is confident that the recovery of the tourism industry will be faster than expected, provided that the global economy does not experience a significant downturn affecting public income, and there are no new large-scale deadly pandemic outbreaks.
Kim Jong-un's father and daughter have a close interaction, while his wife is left alone on the side
(Xinhua, Nov. 11) On the evening of the 8th, North Korea held a military parade to mark the 75th anniversary of the founding of the People's Army. Not only did Kim Jong-un's daughter, Kim Jai-ae, attend the event, but she also stood on the main platform alongside her father.
North Korean state media on the 9th released a video, showing Kim Jong-un embracing his daughter, Kim Yo-jin. She repeatedly looked at him with warm eyes and whispered to him. She gently cupped his face with both hands and even touched his chest. The touching scenes of father and daughter were broadcast nationwide.
However, First Lady Li Xuezhuan could only step back aside.
According to reports from South Korean "Chosun News" and other media, in the official photo of Kim Jong-un and Ri Sol-ju and their daughter Kim Jung-aee attending a banquet on the 7th, Kim Jung-aee not only sat in the center C position among her parents for the first time, but on the following night during the 75th anniversary of the Army Day parade, she also joined Kim Jong-un on the main stage.
After the parade, the official media of North Korea, "North Korean Central Television," released the parade footage, revealing more touching scenes of father-daughter affection.
Kim Jong-un several times looked at his daughter on the stage, and the two of them never lost their smiles. They also whispered to each other in each other's ears. Kim Joon-ai once put her hands on her father's cheeks, and Kim Jong-un responded with a bright smile.
Other official media footage also shows that Kim Yo-jong constantly touches her father's face while sitting on an indoor sofa with him. When she walks with her father by the hand, she also touches his chest with her hand.
Meanwhile, First Lady Li Xuezhong could only walk at the back or sit aside, always a few steps away from her husband and daughter.
Korean media pointed out that Kim Joon-ae, the "money donor," has been appearing more frequently in the public eye since her first public appearance on November 18 last year when she tested the Hwasong-17 missile. In the past three months, she has appeared in formal settings five times.
The North Korean state media used the highest forms of respect, such as "dear son" and "honorable son," when referring to Kim Jong-nam. It also aired footage of Kim Jong-nam shouting repeatedly on the stand, looking down at the military parade, vowing to support the "white-haired bloodline" to the death.
These signs have led the outside world to suspect that Kim Jong-un's daughter may have already been designated as the successor to the Kim regime. The Washington Post analyzed that "this is a signal that Kim Jong-un is pushing his teenage daughter into a position of succession." However, some views argue that such a judgment is still premature. With Kim Jong-un having a son, there are still various possibilities.
Chongyang Q&A: How to View the U.S. January Nonfarm Payrolls Data That Surpassed Expectations and Future Trends - Economic Observer - Professional Financial News Website
Q: How does Chongyang Investment view the U.S. January non-farm payroll data, which significantly exceeded expectations, and its future trends?
A: The U.S. Department of Labor released the January 2023 nonfarm payrolls data on February 3. The number of new nonfarm jobs added was 517,000, significantly higher than the market's expected 189,000. The unemployment rate fell to 3.4%, the lowest level in nearly 50 years. Hourly wages grew by 4.4% year-over-year and 0.3% month-over-month, showing a slight slowdown.
From a total perspective, this data is affected by many one-time and technical factors, such as the return of teachers and staff who were on strike, adjustments in the survey statistics口径, and updates to the seasonal adjustment factors. Subsequent revisions may occur (refer to the January 2022 non-farm payroll data, which was revised from 504,000 to 364,000). Therefore, non-farm employment may not be as strong as the data suggests.
From a structural perspective, the impact of layoffs in high-tech enterprises is still present, but the proportion is relatively low; in contrast, sectors with high contactivity such as leisure hotels and education and health services have seen a significant rebound in new job creation. The data shows that there is still a labor shortage in the service sector.
Combining with the rebound in the PMI of the services sector, the rise in job vacancies and the narrowing of the labor shortage, as well as the strong performance of consumer credit data, we judge that after the extreme weather and holidays, the U.S. services sector is experiencing a rapid recovery.
We believe that it is difficult to alleviate the tightness in the U.S. labor market without a decline.
Currently, there is still a supply gap of more than 5 million in the U.S. labor market, and the labor participation rate is rising slowly. Especially for the labor participation rate of people aged 25-54, it has already approached the high level before the pandemic.
In addition, due to the strong savings and consumption credit of American residents, the strong demand for consumption will further delay the impact of tight policies on the job market.
The continued tightness of the labor market supply and demand will be the hallmark of the U.S. economy in 2023.
With the current situation approaching the end of rate hikes and with inflation expectations constantly fluctuating, we are not optimistic about the continued decline in wage growth.
Before the release of this data, the U.S. stock, bond, and currency markets had been overly optimistic about the Federal Reserve's path of rate hikes.
With the continuous improvement of inflation data, the Federal Reserve has been gradually slowing its pace of rate hikes while maintaining a delicate balance of market inflation expectations. However, market participants have ignored the Fed's statements leaning towards a hawkish stance, instead expecting an early halt to rate hikes and even two interest rate cuts by the end of the year.
In the context of tight labor market supply and demand and the uncertain impact of tightening policies, we believe that the Federal Reserve's guidance on rate hikes at the end of the year is relatively reasonable. However, the adjustment of market extreme expectations and the congestion in trading may pose certain harm to the market.
Despite this, the stubbornly high wage growth has not been able to reverse the rapid decline in current inflation. With the M2 data turning negative year-on-year and the gradual improvement of leading CPI sub-indexes, since October the US CPI data has already entered a phase of rapid decline. The stubborn wage growth can only serve as a floor for future inflation data. The current market expectations are difficult to disprove. After the next round of inflation data is released, the market may enter a new round of debate.
Founder of Shanshan Group Zheng Yonggang died suddenly from a heart disease. He once led China's top clothing stock to transform.
Economic Observation Network reporter Zheng Yuxin. On February 11, the WeChat official account of Ssangyong Holding Co., Ltd. (hereinafter referred to as "Ssangyong Holding") issued a notice of mourning. It was announced that the founder of Ssangyong Group and the chairman of the Board of Directors of Ssangyong Holding, Zheng Yonggang, died on February 10, 2023, due to sudden cardiac disease, after failing to recover from treatment. He was 65 years old.
In the 1980s, Zheng Yonggang served as the plant manager of a state-owned cotton mill. When he was transferred, in 1989 he became the plant manager of the predecessor of Shanshan Co., Ltd., Ningbo Yungang Textile Factory.
In December 2022, at the 20th China Enterprise Leaders' Conference and the 22nd China Enterprise Star of the Future Conference, Zheng Yonggang said in his speech that in the Chinese men's suit market in the 1990s, the market share of Shanshan brand reached as high as 37%.
Shanshan Group (600884.SH) went public in 1996, making it the first clothing company in China to list on the stock market.
Just when the fashion business of Shanshan Group was thriving, the company began to transform. This was because Zheng Yonggang realized that "once the market opens up, Chinese brands will naturally form a tiered brand market. It is unlikely that Shanshan suits will be able to maintain their leading position for a long time and become the national clothing for the Chinese people."
In 1999, Shanshan began to study corporate transformation. Zheng Yonggang recalled at that time, "We came to Shanghai to figure out what kind of transformation we should undertake. We specifically sought out many experts and scholars, holding continuous 'Zhuge Liang meetings' and practical discussion seminars."
Finally, we have set two major strategic directions.
The first direction is new energy and new materials, and the other is big health, which has a bright future.
After the strategic direction was determined, Suan Suan began to seek partnerships. At that time, the Carbonaceous Materials Research Institute of Anshan Thermal Energy Institute was working on a national 863 project to support Anshan Iron & Steel. However, the research funding was insufficient. Therefore, Suan Suan and the Carbonaceous Materials Research Institute of Anshan Thermal Energy Institute signed a cooperation agreement to jointly promote the industrialization of the national 863 project "Intermediate Phase Carbon Microspheres." They then engaged in the research, development, production, and sales of anode materials, achieving the commercial promotion of anode materials domestically.
In 2001, Suan Suan Shares achieved the large-scale production of mesophase carbon microspheres and began to replace imported products domestically, becoming the main supplier of mesophase carbon microspheres in China, taking the place of Japan.
This is the first lithium-ion battery anode material production enterprise in China, achieving the domestication of lithium-ion battery anode materials.
With the rapid popularization of lithium batteries, Shanshan Co., Ltd.'s lithium battery business has begun to develop gradually. In 2007, the lithium battery materials business was written into the main business of Shanshan Co., Ltd. The revenue share of the clothing business has been decreasing year by year, until 2013, when the revenue from the lithium battery materials business of Shanshan Co., Ltd. completely surpassed that of the clothing business.
In February 2021, Shanshan Group successfully acquired LG Chem's polarizer business and entered the LCD polarizer market, establishing Shangjin Optoelectronics Co., Ltd. in the polarizer sector. This field has a high level of technical complexity. Shanshan's main competitors are global companies such as Samsung of South Korea, Sumitomo Chemical and Nikko Electric of Japan.
Zheng Yonggang once shared his insights on post-investment management, saying, "The success rate of Chinese private enterprises acquiring some high-tech companies abroad is relatively low. Why do those acquisitions fail?"
"Because everyone will consider this, I have spent so much money, so I need to send people to manage it, right? This is usually a common action," said Zheng Yonggang. However, Zheng Yonggang was different. On the day of the successful acquisition of Sasan, Zheng Yonggang announced to the public that as a major shareholder of Sasan, Sasan would implement a "one enterprise, two systems" policy. Except for Zheng Yonggang serving as chairman, no staff from Sasan Enterprise would be sent to Sasan. Instead, it would be operated through authorized management. The original distribution system, corporate culture, and various management models would remain unchanged.
ICC Xinluo Information released the 2022 negative electrode data. According to the data, Suan Suan Negative Electrode achieved an outbound volume of more than 2 million tons in 2022, a year-on-year increase of 100%, with a market share of 16%. The output of artificial graphite negative electrode materials has remained number one.
According to Yano Research's statistics in the polarizer business, the company's Shangjin Optoelectronics accounted for 29% of the global market share in 2022, and is expected to reach 32% in 2023, maintaining its position as the global leader.
In the first three quarters of 2022, Ssangyong Shares' revenue was 15.841 billion yuan, and its net profit was 2.313 billion yuan. On February 10th, the stock price of Ssangyong Shares was 19.24 yuan per share, with a total market capitalization of 43.6 billion yuan.
On February 9th, Shanshan Shares stated on its interactive platform that as of the end of 2022, the company's effective graphite production capacity was about 100,000 tons. The graphite production capacity of the Meishan project is still in the process of ramping up.
Wande shows that Zheng Yonggang is still the chairman and actual controller of Ssangyong Shares.
The Tianyancha shows that Zheng Yonggang holds 40.1% of the total equity in Ssangyong Holding Co., Ltd.
Li Zhihua is the general manager of Shanshan Co., Ltd. He was born in 1968. He has previously served as the chairman of Shanshan Battery Materials Co., Ltd. in Dongguan City, Shanshan New Materials Technology Co., Ltd. in Ningshan, Shanshan Energy Technology Co., Ltd. in Hunan Province, and vice president of Shanshan Co., Ltd. in Ningshan.
On February 10, Shanshan Shares stated on its investor interaction platform that there are no close family relationships between the company's senior management and its actual controller.
"Fund E Account" Arrives: CSRC Authorizes Development to Enable Aggregation and Inquiry of All Funds under an Investor's Name - Economic Daily Observers - Professional Financial and Economic News Website
China Economic Weekly reporter Chen Shan: Is it difficult to aggregate account information due to the complex channels for purchasing funds?
Always forgetting the "small treasury"?
The issues of the base population will soon be resolved.
On February 10th, the CSRC announced that the "Fund E Account" APP has been launched on various mobile app stores and is now conducting a public trial operation.
According to the introduction, the "Fund E Account" provides a "one-stop" service for individual investors to query information about public offering fund accounts and shares, aiming to address industry service pain points such as investors forgetting their accounts and the inconvenience of querying.
According to data from Wind, as of the end of 2022, there were 156 domestic fund management companies engaged in public offering fund management, with total managed fund scale of about 26 trillion yuan; there were 167 institutions responsible for registering public offering fund shares and 411 sales institutions for public offering funds.
Each share registration institution's account system is independent of each other, and there is a lack of a unified industry query channel.
Some industry professionals stated that as of the end of 2021, the number of fund investors in our country exceeded 720 million, meaning that half of the population in the country has invested in fund products.
Previously, investors had to check their purchase records through different sales institutions. With the "Fund E Account," they can query the purchase details from all sales channels, making it clear at a glance how many funds they hold.
What is the "Fund E Account" APP?
According to relevant documents, the "Fund E Account" APP is a mobile application developed by China Securities Depository and Clearing Co., Ltd. with approval from the China Securities Regulatory Commission. It serves as a portal for the public offering fund industry and provides investors with a one-stop query service for the holdings of all public offering fund products outside the exchange.
Investors can conveniently and efficiently understand the holdings of their offline shares in various publicly offered fund products of different fund management companies through the "Fund E Account," regardless of the sales channels they used to purchase them, thereby significantly improving query efficiency.
Specifically, investors can query the following information through the "Fund E Account": information about their fund accounts opened with all public fund management companies across the market, information about their trading accounts opened with public fund sales institutions, as well as details about their holdings of off-exchange shares in products such as publicly offered securities investment funds and collective asset management plans established and managed by securities companies, where the number of investors is not restricted to 200 people.
Li Yiqian, business director of the Shanghai Stock Exchange Fund Evaluation Research Center, told the Economic Observation Network that investors can use the "Fund E Account" app to learn about all their holdings of off-exchange public funds in one go. This helps them find "forgotten assets." More importantly, it aggregates all fund investment information across accounts for customers, helping them more timely, accurate, and comprehensively understand their own investment situation, thus better optimizing their fund portfolio and better planning their financial arrangements.
How to register and use?
The reporter noticed that the "Fund E Account" app has been uploaded to app stores of major phone brands such as Huawei, Apple, Xiaomi, OPPO, and Vivo. However, the app is still in the public trial operation phase. Registration requires an invitation code, and investors can contact the fund management company of the fund they hold to obtain it.
A customer service representative from a medium-to-large-scale public fund company in Shanghai provided a six-digit invitation code to journalists from Economic Observation Network and told them, "During the trial operation phase of the APP, China Securities Depository and Clearing Co., Ltd. provides each fund manager with a certain number of invitation codes. Investors can call to obtain them, and we will provide them."
After the trial operation is completed, users will no longer need an invitation code when registering.
In this challenging era, maintaining a positive mindset and an optimistic attitude is essential. No matter what difficulties we encounter, we should believe in our ability to overcome them. A positive mindset not only helps us face challenges but also enhances our confidence when we achieve success. Therefore, cultivating a positive mindset and an optimistic attitude is something that everyone should value.
According to the "Fund E Account" download and usage instructions published on the CSRC website, the journalist registered the account and completed the query process, which took only a few minutes. The entire process mainly consisted of four steps: user registration, real-name verification, fund account binding, and public fund inquiry.
After completing the real-name verification step, click the "Assets" fund binding button. All the fund products purchased by the journalist through various fund sales platforms will be displayed in the binding options list (available for binding). After completing the binding, you can clearly see the total assets of the invested funds and start using the query functions.
It is worth noting that the "Fund E Account" only provides query services for investors and cannot be used for subscription, redemption, or trading.
"Fund E Account" displays data that is generally three trading days before the date on which the user queries (T day).
For example, on February 10th, when you open the "Fund E Account," the total assets, number of fund shares, and fund net value displayed on the asset page correspond to the date three trading days before February 10th, i.e., February 7th.

On Saturday (February 11), French unions once again launched a nationwide strike to oppose the government's pension reform proposal, urging citizens to take to the streets to express their opinions.
This is the fourth day of the social movement opposing the government's draft pension reform plan.
It may affect the next steps of the labor unions depending on how many people respond to the rally's call.
This is the fourth time in the past month that France's eight major trade unions have jointly launched a nationwide strike. It is also the first time that the protest has scheduled demonstrations on Saturday.
Given that Saturday is a day off for most French people, they do not need to choose between participating in protests or striking by withholding wages. Therefore, union groups hope that today's assembly will attract more people to participate.
The unions of the public transportation system, subways, and state-owned railway companies did not call for strikes today. Therefore, the public transportation system should operate normally, allowing citizens to take the bus to the gathering location. At the same time, in areas where schools have started their winter break, people can normally begin their holiday trips.
The union groups hope that more than one million people will attend the demonstration today, while the police expect the national participation to be between 600,000 and 800,000, with around 90,000 to 120,000 people gathering in Paris city center.
Since the anti-government protest against the pension reform plan began, although polls show that the majority of French people do not support the measure of delaying the statutory retirement age from the current 62 years to 64 years, the number of people who have actually gone to the streets to participate in the protests has gradually decreased.
The first strike demonstration on January 31 gathered between 1.27 million and 2.5 million people nationwide.
The crowd of protesters on Tuesday (February 7th) has shrunk to between 750,000 and 2 million nationwide.
The pension reform plan is currently under discussion in the French National Assembly.
Opposition parties, especially the radical left-wing faction, have submitted countless amendment proposals, causing the deliberation process to proceed very slowly.
The debates between the court and the people have become increasingly intense and sharp.
A member of the far-left party, La France Insoumise, recently posted a photo on social media of himself standing on a balloon with the portrait of the Minister of Labor, sparking protests from the ruling party.
The Speaker of the National People's Congress finally announced a ban on the member of the legislature from participating in parliamentary activities for 15 days as a penalty for this behavior.
Although this reform plan has sparked the largest social upheaval in France in the past two decades, so far the government has made only minor adjustments to the draft and has not made any significant concessions.
The union has prepared to launch another action on Thursday, the 16th. On March 7th, when the reform proposal was submitted to the Senate for discussion, the union also planned to organize a protest.
China Encourages Higher Birth Rates, Universities Urge Donations of High-Quality Sperm
On the 9th, the Human Sperm Bank in Yunnan Province, China, issued a publicity campaign seeking donations from individuals with a college degree or higher or students. Those who successfully donate sperm 8 to 12 times can receive a subsidy of 4,500 RMB.
According to reports by Taiwan media today, the information topped the Weibo hot search list on that day and triggered initiatives from many provinces and cities, including Zhejiang, Shaanxi, Hubei, Shandong, and Beijing.
According to a report by the Free Times with a byline, China encourages college students to donate sperm.
Universities across the country offer sperm donations, with Beijing having the highest requirements. In addition to educational background, they also require a height of over 170 centimeters, a well-proportioned face, no obvious hair loss, and a myopia degree not exceeding 600 degrees.
According to the report, Yunnan Human Sperm Bank stated that donors can receive a full set of male reproductive health examinations worth 1,000 RMB. "Special emphasis is placed on semen and chromosomal tests, which most people have never undergone. These tests are significant for understanding one's fertility status," it said.
The donor must meet the following conditions: height of 165 centimeters or above, college education or higher, or currently enrolled in school; no color blindness or color weakness, and no genetic diseases or infectious diseases such as hepatitis B.
Reports cited China's Tianmu News, stating that the Shaanxi Human Sperm Bank has posters with the slogan "Paid sperm donation is a glorious thing," urging young people to respond to the call for sperm donation, and encouraging netizens to forward the related information, "to spread love."
The price in Shaanxi Province is higher than that in Yunnan. Qualified donors can receive 300 RMB in cash each time. Donors who complete a certain number of donations can receive a subsidy of 5000 RMB.
The current highest price is in Beijing, with the highest subsidy of 5150 RMB.
The Last Communist Prime Minister of East Germany Dies Today
The successor of the East German Communist Party reported in a statement, "Last night, Hans Modrow left us at the age of 95."
Therefore, our party has lost a prominent leader.
This project requires more funding, so we need to apply for additional funds from the government.
Four days after the Berlin Wall, which had divided East Berlin and West Berlin for 28 years, was opened on November 13, 1989, Modrow said he would help turn East Germany into a democratic country.
At that time, communist leaders were forced to step down due to protests across East Germany demanding democracy and freedom.
Mikhail Gorbachev, when he came to power in the Soviet Union, required reforms in Eastern Europe under Soviet domination.
In March 1990, Modrow announced that the German Democratic Republic (GDR) would hold its first and only free election.
The election results led to his resignation as prime minister.
Although Modrow carried out reforms, opponents accused him of trying to delay the political changes and unification that took place in October 1990.
He also faced criticism for trying to reform rather than abolish the secret police.
In 1993, Modrino was convicted of election fraud in the municipal election of May 1989, but he was not imprisoned.
Some claim that these accusations are politically motivated.
From 1990 to 1994, Modrow continued to serve in the German Parliament and served as a member of the European Parliament from 1999 to 2004.
Modro sees himself as a reformer and hopes to change the Communist Party from within.
In 1999, he told Reuters that he did not want the former East Germany to return, but he said that the achievements of East Germany should be acknowledged.
Modrow was born in 1928 in the German town of Jasenitz (now part of Poland's Jasienica) and received training as a mechanic.
During World War II, he served as a platoon leader in the youth fire department and later joined the Nazi Volkssturm organization, which conscripted men aged 16 to 60 for the final struggle of the war as the conflict was about to end.
At the age of 17, Modro was captured by the Soviet army and taken to the Soviet Union as a prisoner of war, where he attended anti-fascist courses and became a firm Communist Party member.
In 1949, the same year East Germany was established, he returned to his country and found a job as a mechanic. He continued to study social sciences and economics and eventually earned a doctoral degree.
Modrow was a staff member of the Free German Youth Movement. Almost all young people in East Germany joined the movement, and by the time of the ruling Socialist Unity Party (SED), Modrow became the regional party chairman of Dresden from 1973 to 1989.
He has served in the East German parliament for more than 30 years.
In 1990, Modrow became the de facto leader of East Germany and held the highest national position.

Strengthen restrictions on technology exports to China
According to a report by The Wall Street Journal today, following the balloon incident, the United States is preparing to further tighten technical exports to China.
Current and former security officials said that the above balloon incident highlighted the national security threat posed by China and will help gain more political support for strengthening U.S. technological protection.
The United States said the balloon carried antennas and sensors used for intelligence gathering and communication.
According to Martijn Rasser, a former senior official at the Central Intelligence Agency and currently a researcher at the Center for Strategic and International Studies in Washington.
"This is undoubtedly fueling the fire and supports the views of the hardliners against China."
White House Press Secretary Karine Jean-Pierre said on Thursday, "We will also consider taking broader actions to expose and address China's more extensive surveillance activities, which pose a threat to our national security and to our allies and partners."
"U.S. officials said they have informed the governments of more than 30 countries about the incident involving the balloons. International coordination is crucial for addressing the threat from China."
Emily Benso, a senior researcher at the Center for Strategic and International Studies, said that in addition to the actions of the Biden administration, the newly established U.S. Congressional Committee on Strategic Competition with the Chinese Communist Party may also pressure the United States to take more stringent measures to slow down China's development.
She said that members of Congress will feel increasing pressure to take a harder line, which in turn may force the White House to adopt a tougher policy.
The Wall Street Journal reported that many people who advocate for stricter export controls are also pushing to expand the scope of controls, including industries such as artificial intelligence.
The U.S. government has also already begun developing an external investment review plan to examine whether U.S. investments overseas may pose potential national security threats from China.
Analysts say that the White House may face calls for the plan to be implemented more quickly.
Continuous efforts in rescue and relief operations after the Syria-Turkey earthquake
Since a strong earthquake struck Turkey and the adjacent Syria, rescue teams from dozens of countries have been working day and night in the ruins of thousands of collapsed buildings, desperately saving survivors buried under the debris.
In cold temperatures, they often urge people to stay quiet, as they desperately try to hear any sound of life from the run-down concrete piles.
According to a report by Reuters today, two more women were rescued after surviving for several days in the earthquake ruins.
However, the number of deaths caused by the earthquake has risen to 24,150.
The number of deaths in Turkey is 20,665, and more than 3,500 people have died in Syria.
The 7.8-magnitude earthquake that occurred on Monday continued to trigger several strong aftershocks in Turkey and Syria. This is the seventh deadliest natural disaster of this century, surpassing the earthquake and tsunami in Japan in 2011 and approaching the 31,000 deaths caused by the earthquake in neighboring Iran in 2003.
The similar strong earthquake that occurred in the northwest of Turkey in 1999 caused more than 17,000 deaths.
The Turkish authorities said on Saturday that after the deadliest earthquake in the region in 20 years, two women were trapped for 122 hours. Rescuers in Turkey have rescued the two women from the rubble of a collapsed building.
A 55-year-old man who was injured was confirmed to be Masallah Cicek. He was rescued from the ruins of a collapsed building in Diyarbakir, the largest city in southeastern Turkey.
In cold temperatures, rescuers often urge people to stay quiet because they are desperately trying to hear any sounds of life from the crumbling concrete piles.
In the Samandag district of Turkey, rescuers crouched beneath a concrete slab, quietly saying "Inshallah" — "God bless" — as they carefully extended their hands into the ruins to rescue a 10-day-old newborn.
He widened his eyes, watching the infant Yagiz Ulas, wrapped in a warm blanket, being carried to the field hospital.
The video image shows that emergency personnel also took away his mother, who was dizzy and pale on the stretcher but conscious.
Fuat Oktay, Turkey's Vice President, told reporters overnight that in the past 24 hours, 67 people have been rescued from the rubble, and 31,000 rescue personnel have been deployed in the affected areas.
He said that about 80,000 people were receiving treatment in hospitals, and 1.05 million people were homeless due to the earthquake, crowded in temporary shelters.
Increasing numbers of people are questioning the responses of the two leaders to disaster relief, as many people lack food in cold winter conditions.
According to Syrian state media, President Bashar al-Assad and his wife Asma traveled to a hospital in Aleppo, reports say this is the first time he has visited the disaster area since the earthquake.
Earlier, the World Food Programme said that due to the complicated situation caused by the war, its stockpile in northern Syria under the control of the rebels is about to run out.
On Friday, Erdogan visited Adapazarani Province in Turkey, where he admitted that the government's response was not fast enough as expected.
Opponents have used this issue to attack Erdoğan, who is running for re-election in the vote scheduled for May 14. However, the election may be postponed due to the disaster.
Radio Free Europe/Radio Liberty said that anger over the delayed provision of aid and the failure to launch rescue operations may affect the election.
Even before the earthquake occurred, the vote was seen as the most serious challenge to Erdogan's 20-year rule.
Erdogan called for unity and condemned what he called "negative activities carried out for political interests."
The leader of Turkey's main opposition party, Kemal Kiliçdaroğlu, criticized the government's response.
Critics said, "The earthquake was huge, but even more severe was the lack of coordination, lack of planning, and incompetence."

What exactly was the "object" that fell in the skies of Alaska?
"扑朔迷离，猜测纷纷" can be translated as "Mysterious and confusing, with widespread speculation." or "The situation is so ambiguous that there are countless guesses and speculations."
On Friday (February 10), the U.S. military again used one of the United States' most advanced stealth jet fighters, the F-22, to shoot down an unidentified object flying over Alaska.
The location where the aircraft was shot down was ten miles off the Alaskan coast.
This is the second time the U.S. military has shot down a flying object within less than a week.
Last Saturday, a spy balloon from China drifted over U.S. airspace for several days and was destroyed near the coast of South Carolina in the United States.
The Pentagon said that the second object was first entered U.S. airspace on Thursday night. It was tracked by U.S. military forces as it moved over the northeastern part of Alaska, flying at an altitude of 12,000 meters (40,000 feet). The object was about the size of a small car, much smaller than the previous Chinese spy balloon that was shot down.
The Pentagon also believes there is no indication currently that the object is controllable.
White House National Security Council Strategist John Kirby said on Friday that although the exact appearance of the object flying at high altitude is unclear, it "poses a reasonable threat to the safety of commercial aviation."
Kobe said that this object is different from the spy balloon found in the United States, as it does not carry a "payload."
Kobe said, "We call it an 'object' because it is the best description we can currently make."
Biden listened to advice from the Pentagon before ordering the strike, and he made the decision "very carefully," said科比.
The official description of this object in the United States can be roughly summarized as: it is about the size of a car, has no steering wheel, no suspension system, and flies at an altitude of around 10,000 meters.
But this information still cannot answer basic questions, such as what this object is, which country it belongs to, and what mission it is carrying out.
Currently, many analyses and speculations on the internet suggest that this may be another Chinese balloon.

According to this plot, the whole book is generally light-hearted. When Xiao Yu was still in the online game, even though there were intrigues among guilds, they were often made less serious by the "Xie Shen" (a term used to refer to Xiao Yu's exceptional skills or reputation), so they didn't feel heavy or dramatic.
As for the part about the competition, it conveys a bit of bittersweet feeling about the changes of the times and the transition from old to new (perhaps I am just easily moved).
Reading the whole book took up a lot of study time during my senior high school year, but I couldn't stop myself.
The portrayal of characters and emotional lines in "Full-time" is truly impressive. Even characters who are only briefly mentioned can leave a deep impression on readers. For example, key characters like Liu Xiao Tian, Su Mu Chong, and Yuexian have a large number of fans. Even secondary characters such as Jiang Botao and Chu Yunxiu are very likable. In a game-themed romance novel, it's rare to find a character that readers really dislike. This is a success of "Full-time" in character development.
As for the romantic lines, full-time does not have the common criticisms of general net novels, such as the "three million beautiful women" or the "corrupt" elements. The romance of the entire text has one common point: everyone likes the game "Honor," and most of them want to win honorably and uprightly.
Little measurement has nothing to do with any big conspiracy; everyone looks so simple and cute.
This might also be why full-time jobs don't have many annoying roles.
The full-time IP is very large, and this is also thanks to that.
Setting aside the cultural phenomenon of online novels, "Full-time" is certainly a good book.
He really does give people some passion and a little bit of touching feeling.

The medium version is approximately 36 centimeters long and 23 centimeters wide. This version is the smallest one and comes in a two-volume set with a book case.
Personally, I think it's acceptable. First, the price is affordable, and the 700-plus pages are divided into two volumes, which is convenient for reading as a paperback. 3. Compared to Michelangelo's manuscripts, Leonardo's manuscripts contain much more content.
The former is already a genius. The latter's breadth of knowledge and achievements are so extensive that it is impossible to describe them as merely a genius.
If you only looked at the paintings, you could only grasp half of Da Vinci's genius. But once you see the manuscripts, you'd be completely speechless.

Not very recommendable to read
It was read a long time ago, and I happened to see it. Let me write down some thoughts based on my memory.
This book is more of a collection of fragments about love in life rather than a short story collection.
The tone of this book is extremely plain, like plain water, and the language is simplified to its very essence. The plot is as quiet and uneventful as the streets on a summer afternoon.
Each short story is a snapshot of a brief moment in ordinary life, with no beginning and no end.
Finally, they were collected together and named "What Are We Talking About When We Talk About Love?"
I think this collection of stories and its title are like a form of performance art. The author wants to convey that love is not just the tragic and romantic scenes on stage, not just the eternal vows in urban legends, nor just the sweet and clingy love that's all about being inseparable. Love is not just these things, and it should not be represented by them. Love is also the ordinary and quiet life, the monotonous day after day, and also the helplessness and confusion in facing life, the escape from reality, and the uncertainty about the future.
When you read this book, you may imagine the arguing couple next to you, the dull marriages of people you know, and unsatisfying relationships.
This is exactly why I don't recommend reading this book. From an aesthetic perspective, the writing and plot are dull and uninteresting. Unless your life is full of dramatic ups and downs or as sweet as living in a candy castle, you will feel indifferent and bored while reading it.
In terms of ideological depth, the daily plot does not really impress or expand one's thinking or perspective (a point different from Gabriel García Márquez's "Love in the Time of Cholera"). The brief scenes also do not explore any issues, such as what kind of mistakes led to the failure of love?
What are the reasons behind the breakdown of the main character's marriage?
So I don't recommend reading this book. Whether you read it or not, it doesn't really make much difference. You think what parts of the book might not be suitable for you? Maybe it's the things mentioned in the book, such as those social theories or explanations of human behavior, which might not be suitable for you. I see. The author's writing style is quite academic, which might be difficult to understand, and some parts of the book might be a bit lengthy. The author's ideas are more expressed through techniques and genres rather than plot, which is also the reason why the reading experience of this book is somewhat poor.
Suitable for skimming, not suitable for close reading.
The book has good logic, but the content is not sufficiently rich.
Many sales theories have been explained, including what they are and why they are important, but the part about "how to do it" is quite weak. It only points out the direction but does not provide specific methods or actions.
Moreover, the book feels as though it has been put together piece by piece.
Examples should include both domestic and foreign cases; they should involve both well-known entrepreneurs or salespeople and those who are not well-known.
It's unclear whether these stories were compiled as examples. If the author provided some cases based on his own experiences, it would be more convincing.
Switching between "customer" and "client" in the title.
The title still uses "customer," but it becomes "client" in the main text.
There are also issues such as typos, missing punctuation marks, and numbering errors in the e-book.
Overall, this book is a good choice as an introduction to theory, but it is not suitable for those who are already working in sales and want to improve their sales skills.
Quick Guide to Internet Finance
This book is quite introductory and can be considered as a basic course. It can help everyone quickly get a general understanding of internet finance. However, if you want to delve deeper, you need to follow the outline and explore further on your own.
Moreover, the book was written relatively early. The content about the later Alipay and WeChat "subsidy war" that cultivated user habits has not been mentioned. Now, the share of third-party payments may not be as small as what the book described.
Now many people just bring their phones when going out to eat, and merchants have abandoned UnionPay because of the high bank transaction volumes.
But I still greatly appreciate the author for writing this introductory book. It's clear that the author has years of experience in observing finance and is very familiar with the development history of finance both at home and abroad, which is why the content can be written in such an easy-to-understand way.
It's easy to write something grand and lofty, but difficult is to write in a way that is simple and easy to understand.
I admire you, and more than anything, I want you to know!
The first factor that determines a person's job satisfaction is not the level of salary, but whether they are given enough attention and recognition in their work.
According to Maslow's hierarchy of needs theory, after basic survival needs are met, the most urgent need is the satisfaction of psychological needs—feeling understood, appreciated, and recognized.
Indeed, for the Chinese people, most of them feel "I love you but can't say it out loud." Even if they have love, gratitude, and admiration, they often keep these feelings hidden in their hearts and don't express them.
The author outlines five languages of appreciation in this book: words of affirmation, quality time, service, gifts, and physical touch.
I think what's important is not that you know exactly what the five languages of appreciation are, but that you can genuinely use these five languages to appreciate and be grateful for people around you at any time, whether they are family members, friends, or colleagues.
Actually, the closer the relationship, the more appreciation and gratitude are needed, which can make our lives better.
Learning to appreciate employees can help companies retain talent to a certain extent and reduce turnover rates.
Thank you to the translator for bringing us the fascinating ideas from abroad and the world.
Due to differences in cultural backgrounds between the East and the West, languages will inevitably have some differences. If we have the opportunity to see the original version, we would probably gain more insights.

Mediocre products that fall short of expectations
Advantages:
1. A big brand with cheap prices.
Advantage 2: The motor and blade are both guaranteed by PANASONIC, and they work okay, but they fall short of my expectations.
Disadvantages:
1. There is no indicator light for charging, which means you've even omitted the most basic power indicator. For customers who paid 300 yuan for a product, in the eyes of Panasonic, this is like giving away charity and shows no respect at all.
2. There is a strong odor. Even just charging for 10 minutes, you can already smell the strong smell of burning plastic.
The office had a one-day trial, and it made the whole room filled with this plastic-like smell.
Compared to the Philips PHILIPS model I used before at the same price, although this Panasonic model comes with an extra blade, the quality of this product is really disappointing for consumers.
Small package
When I received the parcel, it said "e-commerce version" on it, so I knew I had fallen into another trap. It's a bit smaller than buying from a supermarket. It should be a M size, and it's different from the packaging shown in the product description. It's called the "new version," after all.
Three stars is because I was careless and didn't check the product specifications carefully. I have no problem with you selling the M size e-commerce version. What does it mean that your product is not as described?
It's a bit tricky in the world of e-commerce. Maybe it's better to just go to the supermarket and buy things.

A frustrating shopping experience
I placed an order during Black Friday, but I waited for a long time and didn't receive it. Later, when I checked, the package had already been delivered. When I asked my colleagues around me, they all said they hadn't signed for it. I immediately contacted the courier company, but they said I had to contact the sender to initiate the query. I then contacted Amazon customer service, and after waiting another ten days, I finally figured out that the package had been mistakenly delivered to someone else. After several rounds of back and forth, I finally retrieved the package. However, the outer packaging had already been opened, and I don't know if the product had been used. Overall, it was really frustrating.

"I haven't opened it yet" or "It hasn't been opened yet"
The battery is working.
"I really want to dissect it to see what's inside."
嗯，用户这段话的意思是说，如果拆开后很失望，花了很多钱就不太值得了；而如果拆开后很满意，再装回去就麻烦了，使用起来就不方便了。所以，用户可能是在担心拆开包装后可能带来的问题，比如失望或者不便。需要把这段话翻译成英文，要准确传达这种担忧和矛盾的心理。 首先，第一句：“后来想想，万一拆开后很失望，花那么多钱就太冤了”可以翻译为：“Later on, I thought about it, and if it turns out to be disappointing after opening it, then spending so much money would have been a waste.”或者更简洁一点：“Later, I thought about it. If it turns out to be disappointing after opening it, then spending so much money would have been a waste.” 第二句：“万一拆开后很满意，再装起来就难了，用着就不爽了。”这部分可能需要更准确的表达。比如：“If it turns out to be satisfactory after opening it, then reassembling it would be difficult, and using it would be inconvenient.” 或者更自然一些：“But if it turns out to be satisfactory after opening it, reassembling it would be a hassle, and using it would be inconvenient.” 可能需要调整结构，让整个句子更流畅。比如：“Later, I thought about it. If it turns out to be disappointing after opening it, then spending so much money would have been a waste. On the other hand, if it turns out to be satisfactory, reassembling it would be difficult, and using it would be inconvenient.” 或者更简洁一点：“Later, I thought about it. If it turns out to be disappointing after opening it, then spending so much money would have been a waste. But if it turns out to be satisfactory, reassembling it would be a hassle, and using it would be inconvenient.” 可能需要更准确地表达“再装起来就难了”和“用着就不爽了”这两部分。比如：“If it turns out to be satisfactory after opening it, reassembling it would be difficult, and using it would be inconvenient.” 或者：“If it turns out to be satisfactory after opening it, putting it back together would be a hassle, and using it would be inconvenient.” 综合起来，可能的翻译是：“Later, I thought about it. If it turns out to be disappointing after opening it, then spending so much money would have been a waste. However, if it turns out to be satisfactory, reassembling it would be difficult, and using it would be inconvenient.” 或者更自然一点：“Later, I thought about it. If it turns out to be disappointing after opening it, then spending so much money would have been a waste. On the other hand, if it turns out to be satisfactory, reassembling it would be a hassle, and using it would be inconvenient.” 可能需要更简洁的表达，比如：“Later, I thought about it. If it turns out to be disappointing after opening it, then spending so much money would have been a waste. But if it turns out to be satisfactory, reassembling it would be difficult, and using it would be inconvenient.” 或者：“Later, I thought about it. If it turns out to be disappointing after opening it, then spending so much money would have been a waste. However, if it turns out to be satisfactory, reassembling it would be a hassle, and using it would be inconvenient.” 可能需要调整用词，比如“拆开后”可以翻译为“after opening it”或者“once it's opened”。“花那么多钱就太冤了”可以是“spending so much money would have been a waste”或者“it would have been a waste of money”。 所以，最终的翻译可能是：“Later, I thought about it. If it turns out to be disappointing after opening it, then spending so much money would have been a waste. However, if it turns out to be satisfactory, reassembling it would be difficult, and using it would be inconvenient.” 或者更简洁：“Later, I thought about it. Worst Online Shopping Experience
After placing an order on September 28th, the system showed that it had been shipped, but I still haven't received the goods by October 12th.
Querying the Amazon order status, the seller did not provide the shipping tracking number, making it impossible to find where the order is.
So I sent an email to the seller asking if they could provide the shipping tracking number. Two days later, there was no reply.
Out of no choice, I called Amazon customer service. The customer service representative provided the seller's phone number. I then called the seller several times, and finally managed to get through. The seller told me that the package was lost in transit and that they could immediately send me a new one.
Although I was very angry, since the computer was urgently needed, I didn't argue much with the seller. Just send it to me as soon as possible.
Result, the next day he called to ask, Have they shipped it yet?
The seller said it hasn't been shipped yet and it can only be sent in the afternoon. I understand. Then I'll wait. Please send it to me in the afternoon.
I called again this afternoon. The seller said they would definitely ship it, and they said they would ship it after 5 or 6 PM. But when I called at 7 PM in the evening, the phone was no longer answered.

Producing defective products, selling defective products, resulting in defective services. They gave a child a watch as a gift with the purchase of six watches. However, one of the watches had incomplete numbers on it, and the times on the six watches were all inconsistent, differing by as much as several dozen hours.
I contacted the manufacturer to ask whether there is a time check when the watch is stored in the warehouse. They replied that "there is." If there is, then why is there such a big difference in time? It must be that the watches lack precision. In simple terms, they are counterfeit products. It's not that I'm being harsh. Look, there are obvious quality issues (the numbers don't display completely), and they are still putting them up for sale. Are they garbage products?
I contacted the seller, who gave a bunch of excuses and even complained that they shouldn't have bought their product in the first place. Such a product, such a seller, and such after-sales service—who would dare to buy things on Amazon in the future?
In short, I suggest you don't buy anything from this seller. Whatever you buy will be junk.

Solar-Powered Sports Watch
Advantages: Solar charging eliminates the need to replace batteries, features world time, multiple alarms, and hourly reminders.
Shortcoming: The mirror is easy to scratch and get dust on it.
Cost-effectiveness: The general price domestically is between 220 and 300 yuan, while overseas purchases cost over 180 yuan (taking about a week to receive).
Very satisfied with this watch
It's definitely genuine. I checked it at the official counter. The appearance of the watch is very nice, the craftsmanship is meticulous, and the materials are sufficient. Plus, it's an imported mechanical movement, so it has a certain weight. This is definitely better than those so-called mechanical watches priced at 300 to 400 yuan. It's truly worth the price.
The watch runs very accurately, with an error completely within acceptable limits. The packaging includes a gemstone certification, and the diamond at the 12 o'clock position on the dial is genuine. It fits well when worn personally and also makes a good gift to give away, as it carries a sense of prestige.

The worst shopping experience I've ever had
On the evening of September 23rd, my wife's phone was dropped. Inspired by the incident, I immediately ordered an 8P on Amazon. There was stock available, but it wasn't from Amazon's own store and there was no price increase (I was secretly happy). Since it wasn't an Amazon自营 product, the shipping was relatively slow, and the system showed that the latest expected delivery date was September 27th.
It's really hard to deal with when my wife can't use her phone. The next morning at around 6 o'clock, she called me and asked me to think of a solution. Later, when I checked JD.com, I saw that 256GB models were available and could be delivered the same day. So I had to order another one right away.
The new one has been purchased, and I just want to cancel the Amazon order right away.
Since the order was placed in the late night, it's impossible for the courier to come to collect it in the early morning. At around 7 o'clock, the user called the customer service to request to cancel the order. The customer service replied that the order has been canceled and asked to wait for the SMS notification.
Well, I'll just wait patiently. By noon, I checked my phone and found that the order was still in the "not shipped" status. I immediately contacted customer service to request the order to be canceled as soon as possible. The customer service replied that they had already processed the cancellation.
By the time it was 3 o'clock in the afternoon, I checked my phone and found the order was still there. There was no choice but to contact customer service again to clearly inform them that this was the third call. Please cancel the order and notify the seller not to ship it!
The customer service representative contacted the seller on my request immediately and informed me that the seller is not familiar with the system and needs to wait until they become familiar with it before proceeding to cancel the order.
But please rest assured that the seller is already aware of this and will definitely not ship the item.
Until Monday, I'll check the order status again. To my surprise, it has already been shipped. I'm really speechless.
Since we can't contact the seller directly, we have to call customer service again.
The subsequent handling was really beyond comprehension. Basically, every step I took, the customer service representative would only proceed with a minimal amount of action.
The order is still in a refused delivery status, and we are preparing to deliver it again!
Won't Amazon follow up on this actively?
The seller's company has already received the money, so it doesn't matter if they don't follow up.
In summary, large items will no longer require prepayment, and this is too troublesome.
It looks very beautiful.
I've been eyeing this camera for a long time, but I didn't buy it because there were some other reasons besides the price. This time, however, since it was available with free shipping and the price was also acceptable, I decided to purchase it.
After buying it, there were no other problems. The issue was when connecting to the software; I couldn't connect no matter how many times I tried. My home router is from TP. After searching online, I found that many people have this problem. I spent more than two hours trying different settings on the router. Finally, I used 360WIFI as a medium to fix it. During this process, I called Xiaomi customer service, but they didn't have any solutions either. They just told me to contact Amazon for after-sales service.
Another point worth noting is that I saw comments from users online saying that this camera supports storage cards starting from 8GB in size, and smaller capacities may not be recognized. Please be aware of this point.
It feels acceptable to use, the picture quality is acceptable, but the sound quality is very poor. The machine also heats up to a certain extent. If it is kept running continuously, this level of heat is a bit hard to accept.
In summary, this product still has many shortcomings. I hope everyone will be cautious when purchasing it.
Why I gave it 3 stars is because this was bought for my first love in life. We dated for almost three years, and he passed away last year. It's still with me to this day.
The reason I gave it three stars is that it was bought during my first love. Now I'm not sure how much I've used it—maybe I've already thrown it away!
We have been dating for almost three years. She left me last year. Now, whenever I think about her, I feel so painful that I want to leave this world. She was my first love, and she has left me with lifelong shadows and heartache. I have not been able to move on from this yet.
When we bought this phone, we thought that even though there were two different feelings at the time, we could still go through good and bad together. But now it's hard to look back. Maybe life is like this. Some people don't want to meet them in their entire lives, and some things are never meant to be known. But what can we do about it?
Every time I see a Samsung phone, my mind immediately thinks of her. A sense of pain floods my body. I have never been willing to spend more than 2,000 yuan on a phone, but when I bought it for her, I didn't hesitate. How kind you can be to someone, but one day when she betrays you, all your vows and promises vanish like smoke.

Companies without the strength can absolutely not do this and also dare not do it.
